VALENTINA (controlled, quiet): "You did. Six Christmases ago."

VOICE NOTES: NATE — low, clipped American English, warmth held under restraint; VALENTINA — warm mid-range American English, controlled, dignity under pressure.

STYLE: prestige cinematic short-drama, photoreal, 35mm anamorphic glass, shallow depth of field, fine film grain, natural skin texture, muted teal-and-amber coastal palette

RULES:
- Generate the longest duration available (10 s if enabled; if the model caps at 8 s, generate 8 s and let post-production extend to 10 s).
- Between 1 and 4 shots: follow the SHOT PLAN and TIMELINE exactly; hard cuts only at the listed times, no dissolves, no fades.
- One line, one shot: whoever speaks is on camera with face and mouth visible for the whole line, looking at the recipient; characters listed as silent keep their mouths closed.
- Silent shots (inserts, reactions, object details) carry no dialogue and never describe speaking.
- The story object from the shot plan stays visible on camera exactly as described; never show the inside of a bag, safe or container.
- Shot 1 starts in motion from the storyboard panel; never open on a static frame.
- Never insert the character reference sheet between scenes.
- Speak only the dialogue above; no narrator, no subtitles or captions.
- No subtitles, captions, overlay text, logos, watermarks or UI.
- Native audio only. NO music, NO score, NO stock sound design.
- Stable faces and hands; no extra characters; no morphing.

NEGATIVE: subtitles, captions, overlay text, watermark, logo, UI elements, frame border, location change, wardrobe change, identity drift, extra characters, morphing faces, distorted hands, slow motion, stock-footage look, music, score, laugh track
